license-key-redirect)\n171\t"}]},"uuid":"5ba71f5f-6a10-4683-be21-cb51d31422d8","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:51.260Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-ray-Desktop-ray-os/0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c/scratchpad/map.md","content":"## Stack & surfaces\n\nTwo independently deployed TypeScript services. **`nextjs/`** is a Next.js 15.5 App Router app on Vercel (React 18, next-intl v4 with 40 locales, HeroUI/Tailwind, Drizzle ORM over `node-postgres`, Better Auth with a magic-link plugin plus a custom `license-key` sign-in plugin, tRPC v10 with superjson, Stripe v20 + a legacy Polar SDK path, Resend for email, Upstash Redis for rate limiting). It is the **system of record for money**: license keys (\"Account Keys\"), the `credit_grants` ledger, the `credit_balances` denormalized cache, and `stripe_processed_events` idempotency table all live here, exposed to the outside world through 23 REST route handlers plus one tRPC catch-all. **`hyperwhisper-cloud/`** is a Bun/Hono service on Fly.io that proxies audio to 11 STT providers and 7 LLM providers; it holds **no database** — it authenticates by POSTing the license key to `nextjs`'s `/api/license/validate`, caches the `{isValid, credits}` verdict in Upstash Redis for 1h (`license:<key>`), and charges usage by POSTing back to `/api/license/credits`. Trust boundaries: the Fly service trusts only the TCP peer address (`fdaa::/16` ⇒ off-edge ⇒ IP is `unknown`); the Next.js service trusts the Stripe signature, an `x-internal-secret` header, a Better Auth session cookie, and — critically — **the bare license key as a bearer credential** for all `/api/license/*` and `/api/account/*` endpoints. Shared mutable state: `inFlightDeductions: Set<Promise>` (cloud, drained on SIGTERM), the `_jwtClient`/`_inflight` Google token singletons, the ~1h in-memory model-list cache in `nextjs/lib/services/model-list.ts`, and the Redis license cache which both services write. Concurrency points: fire-and-forget `deductCredits()` after response flush, `costPromise.then(...)` after an SSE stream, the Deepgram WebSocket bridge, Stripe webhook re-deliveries, and `db.transaction` blocks in `db-layer.ts`.\n\n## Entry points\n\n**hyperwhisper-cloud (Hono, `src/index.ts`)**\n- `GET /health`, `GET /warmup` — unauthenticated, no input.\n- `POST /transcribe` (`src/routes/transcribe.ts`) — raw `audio/*` body; `X-STT-Provider`/`X-STT-Model`/`X-STT-Domain` headers; `account_key`|`license_key`, `language`, `initial_prompt`, `mode` query params read via `rawQuery()`. Buffers up to 2 GB into RAM.\n- `POST /post-process` (`src/routes/post-process.ts`) — JSON `{text ≤100k chars, prompt, account_key|license_key}`; `X-LLM-Provider`/model headers.\n- `POST /assistant` (`src/routes/assistant.ts`) — `multipart/form-data`: `account_key`, `messages` (JSON, ≤6 MB, may contain inline base64 images), `prompt`, `image` file (≤10 MB). Returns an SSE stream from Anthropic.\n- `GET /usage` (`src/routes/usage.ts`) — `account_key`|`license_key`|`identifier`, `force_refresh` query params.\n- `GET /ws/streaming-deepgram` (`src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts`) — WebSocket upgrade; `account_key`, `language`, `vocabulary` query params; binary audio frames proxied to `wss://api.deepgram.com`.\n- `SIGTERM`/`SIGINT` handler — drains `inFlightDeductions` for 4 s.\n- `hyperwhisper-cloud/scripts/smoke-test.ts` — operator-run, hits the live service.\n\n**nextjs — public REST (all unauthenticated unless noted)**\n- `POST /api/license/validate` + alias `/api/account/validate` — `{license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name}`. IP rate-limited 30/min. Falls back to a live Polar API call and can **mint a license + 5000 credits** on a DB miss.\n- `POST /api/license/activate` + `/api/account/activate` — same shape, rate-limited, legacy.\n- `POST /api/license/deactivate` + `/api/account/deactivate` — stub, always 200.\n- `GET /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `?license_key=`; returns balance. **No rate limit.**\n- `POST /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `{license_key, amount, metadata}`; **atomically deducts `amount` credits. No rate limit, no auth beyond the key.** This is the sole write path used by the Fly service.\n- `POST /api/checkout/credits` — `{amount: 5..500, licenseKey?, email?}`; creates Stripe customers and Checkout Sessions. **No auth, no rate limit.**\n- `GET /api/checkout` — redirect to `/credits`.\n- `POST /api/webhooks/stripe` — Stripe signature verified; handles `checkout.session.completed`, `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded`, `checkout.session.async_payment_failed`, `charge.refunded`.\n- `POST /api/webhooks/add-blog-post` — Outrank bearer token (+ optional HMAC); upserts `blog_posts` and calls `revalidatePath`.\n- `GET /api/download` — `?platform=&arch=`; fetches an appcast XML and builds a dynamic `RegExp` from `arch`.\n- `GET /api/config`, `GET /api/internal/models`, `GET /models` — cached public JSON; `/models` and `/api/internal/models` fan out to 6 paid provider list APIs on a cache miss.\n- `GET|POST /api/auth/[...all]` — Better Auth (magic link, `/sign-in/license-key` at 5/min).\n- `GET|POST /api/trpc/[trpc]` — `download.*` (public), `customer.*` (protected), `admin.*` (admin).\n- `GET /api/customer/profile` — Better Auth session required.\n- `GET /[locale]/user/auth/sign-out` — route handler.\n\n**nextjs — internal REST (`x-internal-secret` header, timing-safe)**\n- `POST /api/internal/grant-license` — mints a key + credits for an email.\n- `POST /api/internal/granted-emails` — bulk email dump.\n- `POST /api/internal/licenses-for-email` — keys + balances for an email.\n\n**nextjs — middleware & rendering**\n- `middleware.ts` — matcher `/((?!api|models(?:/|$)|_next|_vercel|docs|.*\\..*).*)`. Gates `/{locale}/user/customers` with a real `fetch('/api/auth/get-session')` + `role === \"admin\"`; gates all other `/{locale}/user/*` on **cookie presence only**; runs `next-intl` for everything else.\n- 22 `page.tsx` files under `app/[locale]/`, plus `sitemap.ts` / `robots.ts`.\n\n## Invariants (ranked)\n\n### I1: Every credit reservation taken at preflight is ≥ the total cost actually deducted for that request, across all retries, fallbacks and re-prompts.\nWhy it matters: the reservation is the only thing standing between a low-balance user and unbounded provider spend the business eats; under-reserving turns each request into a free-money bug at HyperWhisper's expense.\nSearch strategy: `grep -n \"ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS\\|ESTIMATED_ASSISTANT_CREDITS\\|estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks\\|minimumStreamingCredits\"` — then trace each. `post-process.ts` reserves a **flat `1.0`** yet can spend `retriesFor(provider)` primary attempts (up to 3 for groq) + a fallback provider's full retry budget + a *prompt-leakage retry on a third provider* whose cost is `costUsd += retryResponse.costUsd`. `assistant.ts` reserves `3.0 × countInlineImages()` but the multipart `image` file adds an image `countInlineImages` never counts, and output tokens are unbounded. In `transcribe.ts`, cross-check `FALLBACK_CHAINS` against the `rates` array built in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks` — grep `costUsd +=`, `retriesFor(`, `callWithRetry(`.\n\n### I2: Every upstream call that has already cost money results in exactly one `deductCredits()`, on every exit path — including thrown-exception and stream-error paths — and never more than one.\nWhy it matters: a missed deduction is silent revenue loss at scale; a duplicated one double-charges a paying customer.\nSearch strategy: in `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/`, grep `deductCredits(` (7 call sites) and `return c.json\\|return errorResponse\\|return new Response`; for each `return` that follows a successful provider call, confirm a deduction precedes it. Specific suspects: `assistant.ts:350` — `costPromise.then(...)` bills only on resolve, so a rejected `costPromise` (Anthropic stream aborted mid-flight after tokens were billed) drops the charge entirely; `ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` `endSession()` is guarded by `sessionEnded` but is reachable from `onClose`, `onError`, and the Deepgram `close` listener concurrently. Also grep `performDeduction` — any call site bypassing `deductCredits()` escapes `inFlightDeductions` and is lost on SIGTERM.\n\n### I3: A `license:<key>` Redis entry with `isValid:false` is written only for a *definitive* verdict (HTTP 4xx from the licensing API); transient failures (5xx, timeout, network, unparseable body) must leave the cache untouched.\nWhy it matters: `LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600` — one Vercel cold start or a malformed body during a deploy locks a paying customer out of transcription for a full hour across every Fly region.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"cacheLicense(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src`. `middleware/auth.ts:70-79` implements the rule correctly (explicit `if (response.status >= 500) return ... ; // not caching`). `routes/usage.ts:44-52` (`validateLicenseAndGetCredits`) does **not**: it computes `isValid = data.valid === true` after `response.json().catch(() => ({}))` and unconditionally calls `cacheLicense` — so a 500, an HTML error page, or an empty body all poison the cache with `isValid:false`. Verify every `cacheLicense` call is preceded by a status check.\n\n### I4: Every STT adapter that returns a non-empty transcript returns a strictly positive `durationSeconds` (falling back to `estimateSecondsFromBytes` when the upstream duration field is absent or zero).\nWhy it matters: `costUsd` is `durationSeconds × rate` for duration-billed providers, so a missing field means a real, provider-billed transcription is charged **0 credits** — a free-transcription oracle triggered by any upstream response-shape change.\nSearch strategy: `grep -ln \"estimateSecondsFromBytes\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and diff against the 11 STT adapters. Present in `assemblyai.ts`, `mistral.ts`, `soniox.ts`, `openai.ts`, and `google-chirp.ts` (via its own `estimateAudioSeconds`); **absent in `deepgram.ts`, `groq.ts`, `elevenlabs.ts`, `xai-stt.ts`, `azure-mai.ts`**. For each, read where `durationSeconds` is derived (`data.metadata.duration`, `data.duration`, last word `.end`, `data.durationMilliseconds`) and confirm a guard before `compute*TranscriptionCost(...)`.\n\n### I5: Every Stripe webhook handler that mutates licenses or credits dedupes on a stable Stripe id before mutating, and returns non-2xx (so Stripe retries) on any *transient* failure.\nWhy it matters: Stripe delivers at-least-once and fires both `checkout.session.completed` and `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` for the same purchase; a missing guard double-grants credits, and swallowing an error returns 200 and permanently loses the grant.\nSearch strategy: in `nextjs/lib/services/stripe-webhook.ts`, grep `grantCreditLot(\\|grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\\|refundCreditGrant(\\|updateAccountKey(`. `handleCreditPurchase` correctly routes through `grantCreditsForStripeEvent` (keyed on `stripe_processed_events.stripe_object_id`); `handleLicensePurchase:126` calls the **un-deduped `grantCreditLot({sourceType:\"license_bundle\", sourceId: session.id})`** and its only guard is a prior `findAccountByStripeSession` read — check whether `grantCreditLotInTransaction`'s `onConflictDoNothing` target actually covers `(sourceType, sourceId)`. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:121-130`: `handleChargeRefunded` errors are caught and the route still returns `{received:true}` — a refund clawback lost to a transient DB blip is never retried.\n\n### I6: No credit amount, price, or quantity that determines what a user is charged or granted is taken from client-controlled input without server-side re-derivation or a hard bound.\nWhy it matters: this is the direct path to minting free credits or draining another key's wallet.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"body.amount\\|input.amount\\|metadata.credit_amount\\|creditAmount\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib nextjs/server`. `POST /api/license/credits` accepts `amount` straight off the wire (bounded only by `validateCreditDeductionAmount`: finite, >0, ≤1,000,000, 2 dp) and deducts it — with the license key as the sole credential and no rate limit, so anyone holding a leaked key can zero a balance in one request. `session.metadata.credit_amount` is trusted in `handleCreditPurchase`; confirm nothing but `/api/checkout/credits` can write that metadata. Confirm `computeCreditPurchase()` is the only source of `creditCents`/`feeCents` and that `components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx:92` sends only `amount`, never a price.\n\n### I7: Every unauthenticated Next.js route handler whose work fans out to a paid third party or writes a durable object is rate-limited.\nWhy it matters: one cheap POST maps 1:1 to a Polar call, a Stripe customer/session creation, or a 6-provider model fan-out — free amplification and quota/cost burn against an open endpoint.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rLn \"RateLimiter\\|\\.limit(\" nextjs/app/api/**/route.ts` and compare against `nextjs/lib/rate-limit.ts` (only `downloadEmailRateLimiter` and `licenseValidateRateLimiter` exist). `/api/license/validate` and `/api/license/activate` are covered; `/api/license/credits` (GET **and** the deducting POST), `/api/checkout/credits` (creates real `stripe.customers` + Checkout Sessions), `/api/download` (outbound appcast fetch + dynamic RegExp), and `/api/internal/models` are not. For `/api/internal/models` and `/models`, verify the `s-maxage=3600` header plus `fetchAvailableModels`'s in-memory TTL actually bound the fan-out on a cache-busted or cold-lambda request.\n\n### I8: Any handler returning or mutating a specific user's data derives that user from the Better Auth session (or an admin check), never from a client-supplied id; middleware cookie-presence is never the only gate.\nWhy it matters: `middleware.ts` checks only that `better-auth.session_token` **exists** for `/{locale}/user/*` — a forged or expired cookie value passes it. If a page or procedure then trusts that, an unauthenticated caller reads customer data.\nSearch strategy: for each of `app/[locale]/user/(authenticated)/{dashboard,devices,customers}/page.tsx` and `layout.tsx`, grep for `auth.api.getSession`; any page that renders account data without one relies on the presence-only check. Cross-check the tRPC routers: `grep -n \"publicProcedure\\|protectedProcedure\\|adminProcedure\" nextjs/server/api/routers/**/*.ts` — every `admin/*` procedure must use `adminProcedure` (`ctx.isAdmin` from `user.role === \"admin\"`), and every `customer.*` procedure must key off `ctx.user.id`/`ctx.user.email`, never an `input`-supplied id. Also verify `src/db/schema/auth.ts` marks `role` as `input: false` so it cannot be set at sign-up.\n\n### I9: Every comparison of a caller-supplied credential against an env secret is timing-safe **and** fails closed when the env var is unset or empty.\nWhy it matters: `timingSafeEqualSecret(received, expected)` returns `false` when `expected` is falsy — good — but any `===` comparison, or a route that skips the check when the env var is missing, silently opens an admin/mint endpoint in a misconfigured environment.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"process.env\\..*SECRET\\|process.env\\..*TOKEN\\|process.env\\..*KEY\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib | grep -n \"===\\|!==\\|==\"`. Confirm all three `/api/internal/*` routes call `timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)` before any DB work (they do at line ~11 of each). In `app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts`, check the interaction between `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET` and `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN`: when signing is enabled, verify the bearer path cannot be used as an alternative bypass, and that a missing `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` doesn't make `timingSafeEqualSecret` the only barrier to a mint-shaped mutation. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:33-40` correctly 500s on a missing `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` — confirm no sibling handler does the opposite.\n\n### I10: Every `fetch()` to a third-party API carries an `AbortSignal.timeout` or an `AbortController`.\nWhy it matters: a hung upstream leaves an unresolved promise pinning the request scope — on a 1 GB Fly machine that bloats the Bun worker until OOM-kill, and on Vercel it burns the full function max-duration.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"fetch(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src nextjs/lib nextjs/app | grep -v \"signal:\"`. In the cloud service all 10 STT adapters route through `fetchWithTimeout()` in `providers/utils.ts` (default `DEFAULT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000`), but **all 7 LLM provider files — `anthropic.ts`, `cerebras.ts`, `groq-llm.ts`, `gemini-llm.ts`, `mistral-llm.ts`, `openai-llm.ts`, `xai-llm.ts` — use bare `fetch()` with no timeout** (`streamAnthropicChat`'s AbortController fires only on client disconnect, not on a stall). Note `gcs-storage.ts` maintains a *second, duplicated* `fetchWithTimeoutMs` implementation that can drift from `providers/utils.ts`. On the Next.js side check `lib/services/model-list.ts` `apiGet()` and the Polar SDK calls in `src/lib/license-validation.ts` (no timeout is configurable there, yet it sits behind an unauthenticated endpoint).\n\n### I11: In the WebSocket bridge, every acquired resource is released on every path, and closing one side always closes the other.\nWhy it matters: an orphaned upstream Deepgram socket keeps metering billable audio and holds a Fly file descriptor; a leaked `setInterval` survives the request.\nSearch strategy: read `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` end to end and check each early `return`. The `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES` breach at line 308 sends an error and calls `clientSocket.close(1009)` but **never calls `closeUpstream()` / `deepgramWs.close()`** — verify whether the resulting client close reliably fires `onClose`. Also: `pingInterval` is cleared only inside `endSession()`; the `apiKey`-missing early return at line 238 sets it never, but confirm no path sets the interval then exits without `endSession()`. Grep `setInterval(\\|clearInterval(\\|getReader()\\|releaseLock()\\|\\.cancel(` across `src/` — `providers/anthropic.ts`'s `streamAnthropicChat` `start()` catch block never calls `reader.cancel()` when the failure is not a client abort.\n\n### I12: The provider and model reported back to the client (`X-STT-Provider`, `X-STT-Model`, deduction `metadata`) always names the provider/model that actually ran and was priced.\nWhy it matters: `transcribe.ts:669` does `result.source as Provider` and looks the price up from that; if an adapter returns a `source` that isn't its own registry id, or reports a `model` it didn't run, the charge is computed against the wrong rate table and support has no audit trail.\nSearch strategy: `grep -n \"source:\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and check each returned literal against `TranscriptionSource` in `providers/types.ts` **and** against `SttProviderId` in `lib/stt-models.ts` — these are two parallel unions that must stay in sync (`TranscriptionSource` additionally carries `'no_speech'`). Reject anything\n6\t// that does not round-trip at 2 decimal places.\n7\texport const CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE = 2;\n8\t\n9\texport function validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount: unknown): string | null {\n10\t  if (typeof amount !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {\n11\t    return \"amount must be a finite positive number\";\n12\t  }\n13\t\n14\t  if (amount > MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT) {\n15\t    return `amount must be ${MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT} or less`;\n16\t  }\n17\t\n18\t  if (Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount) {\n19\t    return `amount must have at most ${CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE} decimal places`;\n20\t  }\n21\t\n22\t  return null;\n23\t}\n24\t"}]},"uuid":"b5c73e57-773b-4296-a33d-ed0aae9f2c9b","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:55.375Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/app/api/license/credits/validation.ts","content":"export const MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT = 1_000_000;\n\n// credit_balances.balance is numeric(_, 2); amounts with finer precision than\n// the column scale would be silently rounded by Postgres on write, so a\n// sub-cent deduction (e.g. 0.005) can net a zero deduction. Reject anything\n// that does not round-trip at 2 decimal places.\nexport const CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE = 2;\n\nexport function validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount: unknown): string | null {\n  if (typeof amount !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {\n    return \"amount must be a finite positive number\";\n  }\n\n  if (amount > MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT) {\n    return `amount must be ${MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT} or less`;\n  }\n\n  if (Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount) {\n    return `amount must have at most ${CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE} decimal places`;\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}\n","numLines":24,"startLine":1,"totalLines":24}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3fd31b0d-39e8-412c-80b5-a2f97a94ff80","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"c13389d1-bea5-48b1-9c70-f350d25f0015","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a3a2f552e0d9e9db6","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HroPKsCNvHvn2ZRwNAEta9","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n2\t\n3\timport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\n4\timport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n5\timport {\n6\t  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n7\t  computeCreditPurchase,\n8\t} from \"./validation\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n12\t *\n13\t * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n14\t * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n15\t * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n16\t * existing key is the other path.\n17\t *\n18\t * REQUEST BODY:\n19\t * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n20\t * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n21\t * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n22\t *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n23\t *\n24\t * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n25\t * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n26\t * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n27\t *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n28\t *\n29\t * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n30\t * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n31\t * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n32\t * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n33\t * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n34\t */\n35\t\n36\texport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n37\t  try {\n38\t    const body = await req.json();\n39\t    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n40\t      licenseKey?: unknown;\n41\t      amount?: unknown;\n42\t      email?: unknown;\n43\t    };\n44\t\n45\t    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n46\t    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n47\t    if (amountError !== null) {\n48\t      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n49\t    }\n50\t\n51\t    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n52\t      amount as number\n53\t    );\n54\t\n55\t    const siteUrl =\n56\t      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n57\t\n58\t    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n59\t    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n60\t    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n61\t    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n62\t    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n63\t    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n64\t      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n65\t        ? licenseKey.trim()\n66\t        : undefined;\n67\t    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n68\t\n69\t    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n70\t    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n71\t    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n72\t    const guestEmail =\n73\t      !hasLicenseKey &&\n74\t      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n75\t      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n76\t        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n77\t        : undefined;\n78\t\n79\t    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n80\t      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n81\t      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n82\t      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n83\t    };\n84\t\n85\t    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n86\t    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n87\t    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n88\t\n89\t    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n90\t      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n91\t\n92\t      if (!license) {\n93\t        console.error(\n94\t          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n95\t          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n96\t        );\n97\t        return NextResponse.json(\n98\t          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n99\t          { status: 400 }\n100\t        );\n101\t      }\n102\t\n103\t      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n104\t      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n105\t      // them.\n106\t      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n107\t        return NextResponse.json(\n108\t          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n109\t          { status: 400 }\n110\t        );\n111\t      }\n112\t\n113\t      const email = license.email;\n114\t      if (!email) {\n115\t        return NextResponse.json(\n116\t          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n117\t          { status: 400 }\n118\t        );\n119\t      }\n120\t\n121\t      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n122\t\n123\t      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n124\t      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n125\t      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n126\t        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n127\t          email,\n128\t          limit: 1,\n129\t        });\n130\t\n131\t        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n132\t          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n133\t        } else {\n134\t          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n135\t            email,\n136\t            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n137\t          });\n138\t          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n139\t        }\n140\t\n141\t        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n142\t      }\n143\t    }\n144\t\n145\t    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n146\t    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n147\t    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n148\t      mode: \"payment\",\n149\t      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n150\t      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n151\t      ...(stripeCustomerId\n152\t        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n153\t        : {\n154\t            customer_creation: \"always\",\n155\t            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n156\t          }),\n157\t      line_items: [\n158\t        {\n159\t          price_data: {\n160\t            currency: \"usd\",\n161\t            product_data: {\n162\t              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n163\t              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n164\t              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n165\t              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n166\t              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n167\t              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n168\t            },\n169\t            unit_amount: creditCents,\n170\t          },\n171\t          quantity: 1,\n172\t        },\n173\t        {\n174\t          price_data: {\n175\t            currency: \"usd\",\n176\t            product_data: {\n177\t              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n178\t              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n179\t              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n180\t              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n181\t            },\n182\t            unit_amount: feeCents,\n183\t          },\n184\t          quantity: 1,\n185\t        },\n186\t      ],\n187\t\n188\t      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n189\t      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n190\t\n191\t      // Metadata for webhook processing\n192\t      metadata,\n193\t\n194\t      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n195\t      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n196\t\n197\t      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n198\t      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n199\t      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n200\t      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n201\t      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n202\t    });\n203\t\n204\t    // Return checkout URL to client\n205\t    if (session.url) {\n206\t      return NextResponse.json({\n207\t        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n208\t      });\n209\t    } else {\n210\t      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n211\t    }\n212\t  } catch (error) {\n213\t    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n214\t\n215\t    return NextResponse.json(\n216\t      {\n217\t        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n218\t        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n219\t      },\n220\t      { status: 500 }\n221\t    );\n222\t  }\n223\t}\n224\t"}]},"uuid":"3d8a2a65-a3b6-4c7f-b1c6-4cfe446b936e","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:55.899Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/app/api/checkout/credits/route.ts","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\nimport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport {\n  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n  computeCreditPurchase,\n} from \"./validation\";\n\n/**\n * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n *\n * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n * existing key is the other path.\n *\n * REQUEST BODY:\n * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n *\n * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n *\n * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n */\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n      licenseKey?: unknown;\n      amount?: unknown;\n      email?: unknown;\n    };\n\n    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n    if (amountError !== null) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n      amount as number\n    );\n\n    const siteUrl =\n      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n\n    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n        ? licenseKey.trim()\n        : undefined;\n    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n\n    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n    const guestEmail =\n      !hasLicenseKey &&\n      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : undefined;\n\n    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n    };\n\n    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n\n    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n\n      if (!license) {\n        console.error(\n          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n        );\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n      // them.\n      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      const email = license.email;\n      if (!email) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n\n      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n          email,\n          limit: 1,\n        });\n\n        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n        } else {\n          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n            email,\n            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n          });\n          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n        }\n\n        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n      mode: \"payment\",\n      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n      ...(stripeCustomerId\n        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n        : {\n            customer_creation: \"always\",\n            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n          }),\n      line_items: [\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: creditCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: feeCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n      ],\n\n      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n\n      // Metadata for webhook processing\n      metadata,\n\n      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n\n      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n    });\n\n    // Return checkout URL to client\n    if (session.url) {\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n      });\n    } else {\n      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n      },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":224,"startLine":1,"totalLines":224}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c13389d1-bea5-48b1-9c70-f350d25f0015","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

The limit is\n * generous enough for legitimate clients (the macOS app re-validates\n * periodically and many users may share a NAT IP) while bounding abuse.\n *\n * Limits: 30 requests per IP per minute using sliding window algorithm.\n */\nexport const licenseValidateRateLimiter = new Ratelimit({\n  redis: redis,\n  limiter: Ratelimit.slidingWindow(30, \"1 m\"),\n  prefix: \"ratelimit:license-validate\",\n  analytics: true,\n});\n","numLines":33,"startLine":1,"totalLines":33}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2ac103b9-c956-4696-a9b1-b3e2f028cb01","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"2c004c3c-2c40-4dbb-9f93-6c45e14c9fe3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a3a2f552e0d9e9db6","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013hmsxJGbAAAwY1qZ99kjQ1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tconst USER_AGENT = \"HyperWhisper-ModelSync/1.0\";\n2\t\n3\tconst OPENAI_EXCLUDE = [\n4\t  \"whisper\", \"tts\", \"dall-e\", \"embedding\", \"moderation\", \"davinci\",\n5\t  \"babbage\", \"realtime\", \"audio\", \"computer-use\", \"codex\", \"chatgpt\",\n6\t  \"search\", \"transcribe\", \"gpt-image\", \"sora\", \"o1\", \"o3\", \"o4-mini\",\n7\t  \"gpt-3.5\", \"gpt-4-\", \"gpt-4o\", \"gpt-4-turbo\", \"instruct\", \"-pro\",\n8\t];\n9\tconst GROQ_EXCLUDE = [\n10\t  \"whisper\", \"tts\", \"distil\", \"tool-use\", \"guard\", \"safeguard\",\n11\t  \"orpheus\", \"allam\", \"prompt-guard\",\n12\t];\n13\tconst GEMINI_EXCLUDE = [\n14\t  \"imagen\", \"tts\", \"embedding\", \"aqa\", \"gemma\", \"learnlm\",\n15\t  \"robotics\", \"computer-use\", \"deep-research\", \"image\",\n16\t  \"nano-banana\", \"exp-\",\n17\t];\n18\tconst XAI_EXCLUDE = [\"image\", \"embedding\", \"tts\", \"audio\", \"vision\"];\n19\t\n20\texport type Model = { id: string; display_name: string };\n21\texport type ProviderResult = { ok: true; models: Model[] } | { ok: false; error: string };\n22\t\n23\tasync function apiGet(url: string, headers: Record<string, string> = {}): Promise<unknown> {\n24\t  const res = await fetch(url, {\n25\t    headers: { \"User-Agent\": USER_AGENT, ...headers },\n26\t    cache: \"no-store\",\n27\t  });\n28\t  if (!res.ok) {\n29\t    // Log host + path + status only. Never log the full URL (the Gemini\n30\t    // endpoint embeds the API key in its query string) and never log the\n31\t    // upstream body (a provider could echo the request URL/key back in its\n32\t    // error payload). Clients receive only the status code, never the body.\n33\t    let target = \"upstream\";\n34\t    try {\n35\t      const { host, pathname } = new URL(url);\n36\t      target = `${host}${pathname}`;\n37\t    } catch {\n38\t      // keep generic label if url is unparseable\n39\t    }\n40\t    console.error(`[model-list] ${target} -> HTTP ${res.status}`);\n41\t    throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);\n42\t  }\n43\t  return res.json();\n44\t}\n45\t\n46\tfunction sortModels(models: Model[]): Model[] {\n47\t  return [...models].sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));\n48\t}\n49\t\n50\tasync function fetchOpenAI(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n51\t  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.openai.com/v1/models\", {\n52\t    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n53\t  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n54\t  const models: Model[] = [];\n55\t  for (const m of data.data ?? []) {\n56\t    const id = m.id.toLowerCase();\n57\t    if (OPENAI_EXCLUDE.some((ex) => id.includes(ex))) continue;\n58\t    if (/-\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/.test(m.id)) continue;\n59\t    if (m.id.endsWith(\"-chat-latest\")) continue;\n60\t    models.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id });\n61\t  }\n62\t  return sortModels(models);\n63\t}\n64\t\n65\tasync function fetchAnthropic(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n66\t  const all: Model[] = [];\n67\t  let url: string | null = \"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models?limit=1000\";\n68\t  const headers = { \"x-api-key\": key, \"anthropic-version\": \"2023-06-01\" };\n69\t  while (url) {\n70\t    const data = (await apiGet(url, headers)) as {\n71\t      data?: Array<{ id: string; display_name?: string }>;\n72\t      has_more?: boolean;\n73\t    };\n74\t    for (const m of data.data ?? []) {\n75\t      all.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.display_name ?? m.id });\n76\t    }\n77\t    if (data.has_more && data.data?.length) {\n78\t      const lastId = data.data[data.data.length - 1].id;\n79\t      url = `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models?limit=1000&after_id=${encodeURIComponent(lastId)}`;\n80\t    } else {\n81\t      url = null;\n82\t    }\n83\t  }\n84\t  return sortModels(all);\n85\t}\n86\t\n87\tasync function fetchGemini(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n88\t  const all: Model[] = [];\n89\t  let url: string | null = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=${encodeURIComponent(key)}&pageSize=1000`;\n90\t  while (url) {\n91\t    const data = (await apiGet(url)) as {\n92\t      models?: Array<{ name: string; displayName?: string; supportedGenerationMethods?: string[] }>;\n93\t      nextPageToken?: string;\n94\t    };\n95\t    for (const m of data.models ?? []) {\n96\t      const methods = m.supportedGenerationMethods ?? [];\n97\t      if (!methods.includes(\"generateContent\")) continue;\n98\t      const id = m.name.replace(/^models\\//, \"\");\n99\t      const lower = id.toLowerCase();\n100\t      if (GEMINI_EXCLUDE.some((kw) => lower.includes(kw))) continue;\n101\t      if (/preview-\\d{2}-\\d{4}$/.test(id)) continue;\n102\t      if (id.endsWith(\"-001\") || id.endsWith(\"-latest\")) continue;\n103\t      all.push({ id, display_name: m.displayName ?? id });\n104\t    }\n105\t    url = data.nextPageToken\n106\t      ? `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=${encodeURIComponent(key)}&pageSize=1000&pageToken=${encodeURIComponent(data.nextPageToken)}`\n107\t      : null;\n108\t  }\n109\t  return sortModels(all);\n110\t}\n111\t\n112\tasync function fetchGroq(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n113\t  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models\", {\n114\t    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n115\t  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n116\t  const models: Model[] = [];\n117\t  for (const m of data.data ?? []) {\n118\t    if (GROQ_EXCLUDE.some((ex) => m.id.toLowerCase().includes(ex))) continue;\n119\t    models.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id });\n120\t  }\n121\t  return sortModels(models);\n122\t}\n123\t\n124\tasync function fetchXAI(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n125\t  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.x.ai/v1/models\", {\n126\t    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n127\t  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n128\t  const models: Model[] = [];\n129\t  for (const m of data.data ?? []) {\n130\t    const id = m.id.toLowerCase();\n131\t    if (XAI_EXCLUDE.some((ex) => id.includes(ex))) continue;\n132\t    models.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id });\n133\t  }\n134\t  return sortModels(models);\n135\t}\n136\t\n137\tasync function fetchCerebras(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n138\t  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.cerebras.ai/v1/models\", {\n139\t    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n140\t  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n141\t  return sortModels((data.data ?? CACHE_TTL_MS : FAILURE_CACHE_TTL_MS;\n198\t      cache = { value, expiresAt: Date.now() + ttl };\n199\t      return value;\n200\t    } finally {\n201\t      inFlight = null;\n202\t    }\n203\t  })();\n204\t\n205\t  return inFlight;\n206\t}\n207\t"}]},"uuid":"476e7ac5-31e8-4876-8e1b-a7d99c95c82d","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:48:01.615Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/lib/services/model-list.ts","content":"const USER_AGENT = \"HyperWhisper-ModelSync/1.0\";\n\nconst OPENAI_EXCLUDE = [\n  \"whisper\", \"tts\", \"dall-e\", \"embedding\", \"moderation\", \"davinci\",\n  \"babbage\", \"realtime\", \"audio\", \"computer-use\", \"codex\", \"chatgpt\",\n  \"search\", \"transcribe\", \"gpt-image\", \"sora\", \"o1\", \"o3\", \"o4-mini\",\n  \"gpt-3.5\", \"gpt-4-\", \"gpt-4o\", \"gpt-4-turbo\", \"instruct\", \"-pro\",\n];\nconst GROQ_EXCLUDE = [\n  \"whisper\", \"tts\", \"distil\", \"tool-use\", \"guard\", \"safeguard\",\n  \"orpheus\", \"allam\", \"prompt-guard\",\n];\nconst GEMINI_EXCLUDE = [\n  \"imagen\", \"tts\", \"embedding\", \"aqa\", \"gemma\", \"learnlm\",\n  \"robotics\", \"computer-use\", \"deep-research\", \"image\",\n  \"nano-banana\", \"exp-\",\n];\nconst XAI_EXCLUDE = [\"image\", \"embedding\", \"tts\", \"audio\", \"vision\"];\n\nexport type Model = { id: string; display_name: string };\nexport type ProviderResult = { ok: true; models: Model[] } | { ok: false; error: string };\n\nasync function apiGet(url: string, headers: Record<string, string> = {}): Promise<unknown> {\n  const res = await fetch(url, {\n    headers: { \"User-Agent\": USER_AGENT, ...headers },\n    cache: \"no-store\",\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    // Log host + path + status only. Never log the full URL (the Gemini\n    // endpoint embeds the API key in its query string) and never log the\n    // upstream body (a provider could echo the request URL/key back in its\n    // error payload). Clients receive only the status code, never the body.\n    let target = \"upstream\";\n    try {\n      const { host, pathname } = new URL(url);\n      target = `${host}${pathname}`;\n    } catch {\n      // keep generic label if url is unparseable\n    }\n    console.error(`[model-list] ${target} -> HTTP ${res.status}`);\n    throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);\n  }\n  return res.json();\n}\n\nfunction sortModels(models: Model[]): Model[] {\n  return [...models].sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));\n}\n\nasync function fetchOpenAI(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.openai.com/v1/models\", {\n    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n  const models: Model[] = [];\n  for (const m of data.data ?? []) {\n    const id = m.id.toLowerCase();\n    if (OPENAI_EXCLUDE.some((ex) => id.includes(ex))) continue;\n    if (/-\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/.test(m.id)) continue;\n    if (m.id.endsWith(\"-chat-latest\")) continue;\n    models.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id });\n  }\n  return sortModels(models);\n}\n\nasync function fetchAnthropic(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n  const all: Model[] = [];\n  let url: string | null = \"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models?limit=1000\";\n  const headers = { \"x-api-key\": key, \"anthropic-version\": \"2023-06-01\" };\n  while (url) {\n    const data = (await apiGet(url, headers)) as {\n      data?: Array<{ id: string; display_name?: string }>;\n      has_more?: boolean;\n    };\n    for (const m of data.data ?? []) {\n      all.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.display_name ?? m.id });\n    }\n    if (data.has_more && data.data?.length) {\n      const lastId = data.data[data.data.length - 1].id;\n      url = `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models?limit=1000&after_id=${encodeURIComponent(lastId)}`;\n    } else {\n      url = null;\n    }\n  }\n  return sortModels(all);\n}\n\nasync function fetchGemini(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n  const all: Model[] = [];\n  let url: string | null = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=${encodeURIComponent(key)}&pageSize=1000`;\n  while (url) {\n    const data = (await apiGet(url)) as {\n      models?: Array<{ name: string; displayName?: string; supportedGenerationMethods?: string[] }>;\n      nextPageToken?: string;\n    };\n    for (const m of data.models ?? []) {\n      const methods = m.supportedGenerationMethods ?? [];\n      if (!methods.includes(\"generateContent\")) continue;\n      const id = m.name.replace(/^models\\//, \"\");\n      const lower = id.toLowerCase();\n      if (GEMINI_EXCLUDE.some((kw) => lower.includes(kw))) continue;\n      if (/preview-\\d{2}-\\d{4}$/.test(id)) continue;\n      if (id.endsWith(\"-001\") || id.endsWith(\"-latest\")) continue;\n      all.push({ id, display_name: m.displayName ?? id });\n    }\n    url = data.nextPageToken\n      ? `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=${encodeURIComponent(key)}&pageSize=1000&pageToken=${encodeURIComponent(data.nextPageToken)}`\n      : null;\n  }\n  return sortModels(all);\n}\n\nasync function fetchGroq(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models\", {\n    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n  const models: Model[] = [];\n  for (const m of data.data ?? []) {\n    if (GROQ_EXCLUDE.some((ex) => m.id.toLowerCase().includes(ex))) continue;\n    models.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id });\n  }\n  return sortModels(models);\n}\n\nasync function fetchXAI(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.x.ai/v1/models\", {\n    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n  const models: Model[] = [];\n  for (const m of data.data ?? []) {\n    const id = m.id.toLowerCase();\n    if (XAI_EXCLUDE.some((ex) => id.includes(ex))) continue;\n    models.push({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id });\n  }\n  return sortModels(models);\n}\n\nasync function fetchCerebras(key: string): Promise<Model[]> {\n  const data = (await apiGet(\"https://api.cerebras.ai/v1/models\", {\n    Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`,\n  })) as { data?: Array<{ id: string }> };\n  return sortModels((data.data ?? []).map((m) => ({ id: m.id, display_name: m.id })));\n}\n\nconst PROVIDERS = [\n  { name: \"openai\",    envKey: \"OPENAI_API_KEY\",    fetcher: fetchOpenAI },\n  { name: \"anthropic\", envKey: \"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\", fetcher: fetchAnthropic },\n  { name: \"gemini\",    envKey: \"GEMINI_API_KEY\",    fetcher: fetchGemini },\n  { name: \"groq\",      envKey: \"GROQ_API_KEY\",      fetcher: fetchGroq },\n  { name: \"grok\",      envKey: \"XAI_API_KEY\",       fetcher: fetchXAI },\n  { name: \"cerebras\",  envKey: \"CEREBRAS_API_KEY\",  fetcher: fetchCerebras },\n] as const;\n\nexport type AvailableModels = {\n  fetchedAt: string;\n  providers: Record<string, ProviderResult>;\n};\n\nasync function fetchAvailableModelsUncached(): Promise<AvailableModels> {\n  const entries = await Promise.all(\n    PROVIDERS.map(async ({ name, envKey, fetcher }): Promise<[string, ProviderResult]> => {\n      const key = process.env[envKey];\n      if (!key) return [name, { ok: false, error: `missing ${envKey}` }];\n      try {\n        const models = await fetcher(key);\n        return [name, { ok: true, models }];\n      } catch (e) {\n        return [name, { ok: false, error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }];\n      }\n    })\n  );\n\n  return {\n    fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    providers: Object.fromEntries(entries),\n  };\n}\n\n// Per-instance in-memory cache (~1h TTL). Bounds the 6-provider fan-out even\n// against cache-busting query strings, which bypass Vercel's edge cache and\n// hit the function directly. In-flight de-duplication collapses concurrent\n// cold-start requests into a single upstream fan-out.\nconst CACHE_TTL_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;\n// If every provider failed (e.g. a transient cold-start network blip), cache\n// only briefly so a fully-broken response isn't pinned for the full hour.\nconst FAILURE_CACHE_TTL_MS = 60 * 1000;\nlet cache: { value: AvailableModels; expiresAt: number } | null = null;\nlet inFlight: Promise<AvailableModels> | null = null;\n\nexport async function fetchAvailableModels(): Promise<AvailableModels> {\n  if (cache && cache.expiresAt > Date.now()) return cache.value;\n  if (inFlight) return inFlight;\n\n  inFlight = (async () => {\n    try {\n      const value = await fetchAvailableModelsUncached();\n      const anyOk = Object.values(value.providers).some((p) => p.ok);\n      const ttl = anyOk ? CACHE_TTL_MS : FAILURE_CACHE_TTL_MS;\n      cache = { value, expiresAt: Date.now() + ttl };\n      return value;\n    } finally {\n      inFlight = null;\n    }\n  })();\n\n  return inFlight;\n}\n","numLines":207,"startLine":1,"totalLines":207}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2c004c3c-2c40-4dbb-9f93-6c45e14c9fe3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

createMiddleware from \"next-intl/middleware\";\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { routing } from \"./src/i18n/routing\";\nimport { defaultLocale, locales } from \"./src/i18n/locales\";\nimport { sanitizeReturnTo } from \"./src/lib/license-key-redirect\";\n\nconst intlMiddleware = createMiddleware(routing);\nconst localePattern = locales\n  .map((locale) => locale.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, \"\\\\$&\"))\n  .join(\"|\");\nconst LOCALE_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})(\\\\/|$)`);\nconst USER_ROUTE_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user`);\nconst USER_SIGN_IN_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/sign-in`);\nconst USER_AUTH_SIGN_OUT_REGEX = new RegExp(\n  `^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/auth\\\\/sign-out`,\n);\nconst USER_CUSTOMERS_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/customers`);\n\nconst getPathLocale = (pathname: string) => {\n  const match = pathname.match(LOCALE_REGEX);\n  return match?.[1] ?? defaultLocale;\n};\n\n/**\n * Check if Better Auth session cookie is present.\n * For middleware, we only check cookie presence (no HTTP round-trip).\n * Actual session validation happens in the API layer.\n *\n * Better Auth prefixes cookies with \"__Secure-\" in production when\n * baseURL starts with \"https://\", so we check both names.\n */\nfunction hasSessionCookie(request: NextRequest): boolean {\n  return !!(\n    request.cookies.get(\"better-auth.session_token\")?.value ||\n    request.cookies.get(\"__Secure-better-auth.session_token\")?.value\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Get full Better Auth session via API call.\n * Only used for admin routes that need the user's email.\n */\nasync function getBetterAuthSession(request: NextRequest) {\n  const sessionCookie =\n    request.cookies.get(\"__Secure-better-auth.session_token\") ||\n    request.cookies.get(\"better-auth.session_token\");\n  if (!sessionCookie?.value) return null;\n\n  try {\n    const baseUrl = request.nextUrl.origin;\n    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/get-session`, {\n      headers: {\n        cookie: request.headers.get(\"cookie\") || \"\",\n      },\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) return null;\n\n    const data = await response.json();\n    return data?.user ?? null;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Middleware that handles:\n * 1. next-intl locale routing (adds locale prefix)\n * 2. Better Auth session checking\n * 3. User route protection (unified portal for customers and admins)\n */\nexport default async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {\n  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;\n\n  // Check if this is a user route (matches /<locale>/user/*)\n  const isUserRoute = USER_ROUTE_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserSignIn = USER_SIGN_IN_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserAuthSignOut = USER_AUTH_SIGN_OUT_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserCustomers = USER_CUSTOMERS_REGEX.test(pathname);\n\n  // =============================================================\n  // USER ROUTES - Unified portal for customers and admins\n  // =============================================================\n\n  // For /user/customers, require admin access\n  if (isUserCustomers) {\n    const user = await getBetterAuthSession(request);\n\n    // If not authenticated, redirect to user sign-in\n    if (!user) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const signInUrl = new URL(`/${locale}/user/sign-in`, request.url);\n      signInUrl.searchParams.set(\"returnTo\", pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(signInUrl);\n    }\n\n    // Must be admin to access customers page\n    if (user.role !== \"admin\") {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(\n        new URL(`/${locale}/user/dashboard`, request.url)\n      );\n    }\n\n    // User is authenticated and is admin - run intl middleware\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For other user routes (except sign-in, sign-out), check authentication via cookie presence\n  if (\n    isUserRoute &&\n    !isUserSignIn &&\n    !isUserAuthSignOut\n  ) {\n    if (!hasSessionCookie(request)) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const signInUrl = new URL(`/${locale}/user/sign-in`, request.url);\n      signInUrl.searchParams.set(\"returnTo\", pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(signInUrl);\n    }\n\n    // User has session cookie - run intl middleware\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For user sign-in page, check if already authenticated\n  if (isUserSignIn) {\n    if (hasSessionCookie(request)) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const returnTo = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get(\"returnTo\");\n      const redirectUrl = sanitizeReturnTo(returnTo, `/${locale}/user/dashboard`);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(redirectUrl, request.url));\n    }\n\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For all other routes, just run intl middleware\n  const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n  response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n\n  return response;\n}\n\nexport const config = {\n  // Match all pathnames except for:\n  // - API routes (/api/*)\n  // - Raw model inventory endpoint (/models)\n  // - Next.js internal files (/_next/*)\n  // - Static files with extensions (*.*)\n  // - Documentation (/docs/*)\n  matcher: [\"/((?!api|models(?:/|$)|_next|_vercel|docs|.*\\\\..*).*)\"],\n};\n","numLines":161,"startLine":1,"totalLines":161}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"370104ba-ecd7-4826-9e6c-e868b0ae08dc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{ createAuthEndpoint } from \"better-auth/api\";\nimport { setSessionCookie } from \"better-auth/cookies\";\nimport { eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\n\nimport { findAccountByKey } from \"./db-layer\";\nimport { sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect } from \"./license-key-redirect\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/src/db\";\nimport { user } from \"@/src/db/schema/auth\";\n\nexport const licenseKeyPlugin = () => ({\n  id: \"license-key\",\n  endpoints: {\n    signInLicenseKey: createAuthEndpoint(\n      \"/sign-in/license-key\",\n      {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        requireHeaders: true,\n        body: z.object({\n          licenseKey: z.string(),\n          callbackURL: z.string().optional(),\n        }),\n      },\n      async (ctx) => {\n        const { licenseKey, callbackURL } = ctx.body;\n\n        const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n\n        if (!license || license.status !== \"granted\") {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Invalid or inactive license key.\" },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        if (!license.userId) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const [foundUser] = await db\n          .select()\n          .from(user)\n          .where(eq(user.id, license.userId))\n          .limit(1);\n\n        if (!foundUser) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const session = await ctx.context.internalAdapter.createSession(\n          foundUser.id,\n        );\n\n        if (!session) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Failed to create session.\" },\n            { status: 500 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        await setSessionCookie(ctx, { session, user: foundUser });\n\n        const ipAddress =\n          ctx.headers?.get(\"x-forwarded-for\")?.split(\",\")[0]?.trim() ??\n          \"unknown\";\n        const userAgent = ctx.headers?.get(\"user-agent\") ?? \"unknown\";\n        console.log(\n          `[license-key-sign-in] user=${foundUser.id} license=${license.id} ip=${ipAddress} ua=\"${userAgent}\"`,\n        );\n\n        return ctx.json({ redirect: sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect(callbackURL) });\n      },\n    ),\n  },\n  rateLimit: [\n    {\n      pathMatcher(path: string) {\n        return path === \"/sign-in/license-key\";\n      },\n      window: 60,\n      max: 5,\n    },\n  ],\n});\n","numLines":98,"startLine":1,"totalLines":98}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7e02f305-3752-47f0-add2-b315b1ec54c8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

4 segments of 4 chars each for readability\n */\nimport crypto from \"crypto\";\n\n// Base32-like alphabet without ambiguous characters\n// Excludes: 0 (zero), O (oh), 1 (one), I (eye), L (el)\nconst ALPHABET = \"ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789\"; // 31 chars\n\n/**\n * Generates a cryptographically secure license key.\n *\n * @returns A license key in format HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX\n *\n * ENTROPY CALCULATION:\n * - 16 characters from 31-char alphabet = 16 * log2(31) ≈ 79 bits\n * - Each character is drawn uniformly via crypto.randomInt (no modulo bias)\n * - Total effective entropy: ~79 bits (sufficient for license keys)\n */\nexport function generateLicenseKey(): string {\n  const MAX_RETRIES = 3;\n\n  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_RETRIES; attempt++) {\n    try {\n      let key = \"HW\";\n\n      // Generate 4 segments of 4 characters each\n      for (let segment = 0; segment < 4; segment++) {\n        key += \"-\";\n\n        for (let char = 0; char < 4; char++) {\n          // Draw a uniform index in [0, ALPHABET.length).\n          // crypto.randomInt performs rejection sampling internally, so the\n          // distribution is unbiased — unlike `byte % ALPHABET.length`, which\n          // over-represented the first (256 % 31) = 8 alphabet symbols.\n          const charIndex = crypto.randomInt(ALPHABET.length);\n\n          // Validate charIndex is within alphabet bounds\n          if (charIndex < 0 || charIndex >= ALPHABET.length) {\n            throw new Error(\n              `Character index ${charIndex} out of bounds (alphabet length: ${ALPHABET.length})`\n            );\n          }\n\n          const alphabetChar = ALPHABET[charIndex];\n\n          // Final safety check: ensure we got a valid character\n          if (!alphabetChar || typeof alphabetChar !== \"string\" || alphabetChar.length !== 1) {\n            throw new Error(\n              `Invalid character at index ${charIndex}: got ${alphabetChar} (type: ${typeof alphabetChar})`\n            );\n          }\n\n          key += alphabetChar;\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Validate the generated key format before returning\n      if (!isValidKeyFormat(key)) {\n        throw new Error(`Generated key failed format validation: ${key}`);\n      }\n\n      return key;\n    } catch (error) {\n      // If this is the last attempt, throw the error\n      if (attempt === MAX_RETRIES - 1) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Failed to generate valid license key after ${MAX_RETRIES} attempts: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`\n        );\n      }\n      // Otherwise, log and retry\n      console.warn(\n        `License key generation attempt ${attempt + 1} failed, retrying...`,\n        error\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  // This should never be reached due to the throw above, but TypeScript needs it\n  throw new Error(\"License key generation failed unexpectedly\");\n}\n\n/**\n * Validates that a license key matches the expected format.\n * Does NOT check database validity - only format validation.\n *\n * @param key - The license key to validate\n * @returns true if format is valid, false otherwise\n */\nexport function isValidKeyFormat(key: string): boolean {\n  if (!key || typeof key !== \"string\") {\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  // Format: HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX\n  // Where X is any character from ALPHABET\n  const pattern = /^HW-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{4}-[A-HJ-NP-Z2-9]{4}$/;\n  return pattern.test(key.toUpperCase());\n}\n\n=== grep generate ===\nlib/services/license-key.ts:5: * Format: HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX (19 characters total)\nlib/services/license-key.ts:8: * - * Pure validation + pricing helpers for credit purchases.\n *\n * Kept free of Next/Stripe imports so they can be unit-tested directly (see\n * tests/credit-purchase-validation.test.ts) and reused by the checkout route.\n */\n\nexport const MIN_CREDIT_DOLLARS = 5;\nexport const MAX_CREDIT_DOLLARS = 500;\n/** 1,000 credits per US$1. */\nexport const CREDITS_PER_DOLLAR = 1000;\n/** Non-refundable processing fee added on top of the credit value. */\nexport const CREDIT_FEE_RATE = 0.06;\n\n/**\n * Validate the requested purchase amount (whole US dollars, MIN..MAX inclusive).\n * Returns an error string, or null when valid.\n */\nexport function validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount: unknown): string | null {\n  if (typeof amount !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(amount)) {\n    return \"amount must be a finite number\";\n  }\n  if (!Number.isInteger(amount)) {\n    return \"amount must be a whole number of dollars\";\n  }\n  if (amount < MIN_CREDIT_DOLLARS || amount > MAX_CREDIT_DOLLARS) {\n    return `amount must be between ${MIN_CREDIT_DOLLARS} and ${MAX_CREDIT_DOLLARS} dollars`;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport interface CreditPurchaseBreakdown {\n  /** Credits granted: amount * CREDITS_PER_DOLLAR. */\n  creditAmount: number;\n  /** Credit line-item amount in cents: amount * 100. */\n  creditCents: number;\n  /** Processing fee in cents: round(creditCents * CREDIT_FEE_RATE). */\n  feeCents: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Compute the Stripe line-item breakdown for a validated dollar amount.\n * Caller must pass an amount that already passed validateCreditPurchaseAmount.\n */\nexport function computeCreditPurchase(amount: number): CreditPurchaseBreakdown {\n  const creditCents = amount * 100;\n  return {\n    creditAmount: amount * CREDITS_PER_DOLLAR,\n    creditCents,\n    feeCents: Math.round(creditCents * CREDIT_FEE_RATE),\n  };\n}\n=== account/credits alias ===\n/**\n * Account Key credits API (canonical path).\n *\n * \"Account Key\" is the new name for what older native apps call a \"license key\".\n * The wire contract is identical to /api/license/credits — this route re-exports\n * those handlers so new app releases can target /api/account/* while installed\n * macOS/Windows/iOS builds keep hitting /api/license/* unchanged.\n *\n * The JSON field rename (license_key -> account_key) is intentionally deferred\n * to the DB/identifier rename (Part B3); both paths currently speak license_key.\n */\nexport { GET, POST } from \"../../license/credits/route\";","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"fb06dbb0-e588-47f5-a0a5-57712b19309f","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:50:07.510Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"/**\n * Pure validation + pricing assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\nimport test from \"node:test\";\n\nimport {\n  MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT,\n  validateCreditDeductionAmount,\n} from \"../app/api/license/credits/validation\";\n\ntest(\"accepts finite positive deduction amounts within the limit\", () => {\n  assert.equal(validateCreditDeductionAmount(1), null);\n  assert.equal(validateCreditDeductionAmount(150.5), null);\n  assert.equal(validateCreditDeductionAmount(MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT), null);\n});\n\ntest(\"rejects non-finite deduction amounts\", () => {\n  const parsedPayload = JSON.parse('{\"amount\":1e999}') as { amount: unknown };\n\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(parsedPayload.amount),\n    \"amount must be a finite positive number\"\n  );\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY),\n    \"amount must be a finite positive number\"\n  );\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY),\n    \"amount must be a finite positive number\"\n  );\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(Number.NaN),\n    \"amount must be a finite positive number\"\n  );\n});\n\ntest(\"rejects non-positive deduction amounts\", () => {\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(0),\n    \"amount must be a finite positive number\"\n  );\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(-1),\n    \"amount must be a finite positive number\"\n  );\n});\n\ntest(\"rejects deduction amounts above the request limit\", () => {\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT + 1),\n    `amount must be ${MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT} or less`\n  );\n});\n\ntest(\"rejects deduction amounts with sub-scale precision\", () => {\n  // numeric(_, 2) would silently round these on write, netting a smaller (or\n  // zero) deduction than requested.\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(0.005),\n    \"amount must have at most 2 decimal places\"\n  );\n  assert.equal(\n    validateCreditDeductionAmount(12.345),\n    \"amount must have at most 2 decimal places\"\n  );\n});\n\ntest(\"accepts deduction amounts at or above 2-decimal scale\", () => {\n  // The legitimate Fly caller sends 0.1-granularity amounts; these and any\n  // value with <= 2 decimals must round-trip exactly.\n  assert.equal(validateCreditDeductionAmount(0.1), null);\n  assert.equal(validateCreditDeductionAmount(0.01), null);\n  assert.equal(validateCreditDeductionAmount(0.07), null);\n  assert.equal(validateCreditDeductionAmount(12.34), null);\n});\n=== validate route limiter ===\napp/api/license/activate/route.ts:4:import {
